# Astro Knowledge Base — Lagna, Gemstones & the Avatar Throne (Transcript)

Verbatim-style extraction from the recorded class lecture (Sanjay Rath, Jaimini Upadesha), covering gemstones and the lagna, who sits on the lagna, the Avatar and the throne of time, the throne in the zodiac (10th house / Capricorn), Makar Sankranti, the 4th–10th happiness axis, swakshetra and father's-house charts, Sun–Moon domains and the two counting chakras, and mantra/mind worship.

## 18. Gemstones, the Lagna & Intelligence

**18.1 Gemstones for the lagna are about intelligence, not physical strength**
- People always talk about wearing gemstones for the lagna — "make the lagna very strong." But the teacher questions this: if you make the lagna Lord very strong, what do you get? The lagna is for intelligence.
- If you wear a gemstone for the lagna Lord, you think your buddhi (intelligence) will become brilliant — "you will become Einstein by wearing a ring." Why use a gemstone for intelligence?
- His counter-suggestion: do the Gayatri mantra and see your intelligence shoot up. He says the knowledge people attribute to him is not his doing — it is the grace of Mahadev/Shiva, who sits on his lagna.

**18.2 Who sits on the lagna — praise the planet, then the lagna**
- He who sits on the lagna must be praised with Vedic hymns. In the teacher's birth chart, only Shiva (and Guru) sit on the lagna.
- In a general horoscope, whichever planet sits on the lagna must be praised first, and then the lagna itself. If there is no planet in the lagna, then "Jagannath is sitting on your head."

**18.3 Jagannath — the lord of the universe**
- The rule for the lagna is very simple: "It is Jagannath." Jagat means universe; Nath means lord of the universe.
- Saying "Lord of the Universe" raises two automatic questions. Nothing sits on that throne permanently — nobody is permanent there. Nothing is permanent. Thrones are always temporary (gaddi is temporary); even great ministers, kings, and politicians vanish from the throne.

## 19. The Avatar & the Throne of Time

**19.1 The throne of time and the avatar**
- The throne of the world is occupied by an entity called an avatar — he is the occupant of the throne.
- Kali Yuga started in February 3000-something BC. Its start matters because that is the day Lord Krishna sat on that throne.
- That throne is the throne of time, and by accepting it Krishna accepted responsibility as the yuga avatar. For this yuga — many thousands of years — Lord Krishna sits on the throne.

**19.2 Rama to Krishna — the same Hari changes guards**
- Before Krishna, the occupant was Sri Ramachandra. Krishna was born roughly 100 years before Kali Yuga started, but he did not immediately go and sit on the avatar throne.
- When Kali Yuga came, Sri Ram said (loosely): "This dirty Kali Yuga has come — I am not going to look after this, Krishna, now it is yours." The same Rama becomes Krishna.

**19.3 The body is a car; only the Avatar is free**
- The teacher likens the body to a car — "this body is nothing but a car." In a past life he says he was some king; that raja died and he got another body: "climb the car, climb on it, and I was put under bondage (bandi), and I continued."
- This does not happen with the avatar. Nobody can tie him. He is the only entity who is completely free (vimukto).
- All others — devatas too — have some bondage. Varuna: "I am the god of the oceans — what happens when you are on land?" Indra: "I am the lord of the gods — what happens when the asuras are there?" All of them have a bondage; the whole lot of them. The only completely free one is the Avatar.

**19.4 Ram and Krishna are one being — 10 kala vs 16 kala**
- "Ram is Krishna. There is no difference between them" — one and the same entity, just with additional powers.
- As Rama Avatar he had 10 "points" (kala) of power; as Krishna Avatar, 16. They use different words (kala, vibhutis), but the power is far higher — almost complete manifestation. Normally the purna kalas are kept in Vaikuntha/Goloka and not brought down to earth, not even when he sits on the seat.

## 20. The Throne in the Zodiac — 10th House (Capricorn)

**20.1 Where is the throne in the zodiac? — natural 10th house, Capricorn**
- The question: where is this throne in the zodiac? It is the natural 10th house — Capricorn. So Capricorn is the lagna of the throne of Hari.
- Harikula means the family of Hari. Is it a family or a form — is Ram different from Krishna? They are different in manifestation yet the same, because the entity is Hari. Harikula refers to the forms in which Hari has manifested.
- "But where is he sitting? He is sitting in the 10th house of the natural sidereal (the natural zodiac) — that is Capricorn."

**20.2 Makar Sankranti — the awakening of the avatar**
- The celebration: when the sun enters sidereal Capricorn (around 14th January), the day is celebrated as Makar Sankranti.
- The teacher's parampara starts its formal courses on the 14th of January / Makar Sankranti as the starting date, because that is the awakening of the avatar — when the sun enters Capricorn, the avatar will awaken.

**20.3 21 brahmins and why the number 21**
- The Brahmins of Puri, in 21 groups of 21, recite the Gayatri mantra in the morning to awaken the avatar.
- Why 21? Because there are 12 rashis and nine grahas; 12 + 9 = 21. Through the combinations of 12 rashis and 9 grahas, the avatar will be awakened.

**20.4 Veshas — the forms he takes daily**
- Vesha means a form. The avatar takes different forms — Gaja Uddharana Vesha, Hanumanta Vesha (the form of Hanuman), Bankachura Vesha (Vamana Avatar form; chura means the tuft of hair on top of the head, the choti, and bhanka/bent — so bhanka-chuda). Different forms every day.

**20.5 The 10th house is the most powerful Kendra — dashas run from it**
- In this Kali Yuga the avatar sits on the throne of time. Since the 10th house is your time, your dashas actually run from the 10th house.
- "You think the lagna is most important? The lagna is your ahamkara telling you that the lagna is most. The lagna is not the most important house."
- Parashara has made it very clear: among the Kendras are Vishnu Sthanam — the avatars come through the Kendras only, and the 10th is the most powerful Kendra. How could the 10th be most powerful if Hari were not there? So Parashara and the parampara agree: the 10th house is very important.

## 21. Happiness, Rahu in the 10th & the Nadis

**21.1 The 4th–10th happiness axis and gemstones**
- Look at the fourth house and the tenth house — find one benefit, one thing that can make you happy.
- Gemstones come from the Moon, and the Moon is karaka for the 4th house; the 4th house is Cancer. So all gemstones actually come from the 4th house. The 4th–10th house is the axis from which you choose what makes you happy.
- "If you have ensured with all your remedies that your happiness will not get spoiled, the bad dasha cannot function." When choosing a gemstone, ask only one question: "Is this gemstone going to make me happy or not?" If it can make you happy, wear it; otherwise why wear it?

**21.2 Rahu in the 10th takes you on pilgrimage**
- "Even a planet like Rahu placed in the 10th house will take you on pilgrimage — a planet taking you to temples, taking you to the guru, making you meet with us. How can that planet be bad?" The experience at the time may be bad, but that is your dasha, and a dasha has many implications; we wrongly assume only one dasha is working.

**21.3 Three channels — ida, pingala, sushumna; only Saraswati reaches the crown**
- There are three principal channels in the body: pingala (heating), ida (cooling), and sushumna (balancing). Only sushumna goes up to the top of the head.
- "Only Saraswati reaches to the top of the head. Neither Ganga nor Yamuna connects the Mooladhara to it — only Saraswati is connected." We know all this, yet we have all kinds of crazy thoughts and then wonder why we are not happy.

**21.4 Happiness is a function of time; sukha and dukkha**
- Happiness is a function of time; you cannot be happy all the time. But if you are happy a majority of the time, then "you are in sansar, but experiencing some very good spiritual days."
- Spirituality comes through sukha and dukha. If you are sad about losing your car — that was just the car, and you were in love with it. The real car is the body.

**21.5 The body is the real car; jivanmukta is difficult**
- The body is the real car — a two-wheeler (two legs), not a four-wheeler; elephants and cows are four-wheelers, you are a two-wheeler, and this body can take you anywhere you want.
- "My soul is traveling — No, your soul is stuck right in your body; it has not moved from there even for a moment. It is bound with such powerful ropes that you cannot break free."
- To break free from the ropes while being in this body is a miracle; when that miracle happens you are a jivanmukta. "Big gurus are not jivanmukta; rishis could not be jivanmukta. You people talk about jivanmukta so easily." Dattatreya was a jivanmukta; Durvasa was not — he had the full potential of Rudra (he is Rudra himself, Krishna worshipped him as Rudra), yet he was not.

## 22. Swakshetra & Father's-House Charts

**22.1 Nine grahas in their own signs (swakshetra)**
- Draw a blank chart (North Indian, South Indian, or East Indian). Write the planets in their swakshetra (own-sign birth territory) — not moolatrikona, not "office."
- Sun: Leo. Moon: Cancer. Mars: Scorpio. Mercury: Gemini. Jupiter: Pisces (not Sagittarius). Venus: Taurus. Saturn: Capricorn. Rahu: Aquarius (11th house, Kumbha rashi). Ketu: Scorpio.

**22.2 Fathers' houses — the 9th house from each swakshetra**
- Draw a second blank chart and write each graha in its 9th house, because every graha has a father; "grahas don't take birth in the mother's house, they take birth in the father's house."
- From the swakshetra placements: Sun → Aries (9th from Leo). Moon → Pisces (9th from Cancer). Mars and Ketu → Cancer (9th from Scorpio). Mercury → Aquarius (9th from Gemini). Jupiter → Scorpio (9th from Pisces). Venus → Capricorn (9th from Taurus). Saturn → Virgo (9th from Capricorn). Rahu → Libra (9th from Aquarius).

**22.3 Only one royal rashi; Surya is exalted in his father's house**
- Of the 12 rashis, only one is royal — and it is not Leo (that is Western astrology; "Leo is jungle, kings don't live in jungles; if there is a city there is a hillock or high region; the king lives in that high region and looks down on the people").
- Surya is exalted in Aries — "the only planet exalted in his father's house," thrilled to speak of his father, grandfather, great-grandfather: "I am proud of all of them."
- "Surya is the Lord of the zodiac — the whole zodiac belongs to him. I am the king; I am the boss of everything."

## 23. Sun & Moon Domains, Gandanta & the Two Chakra

**23.1 Day and night domains — six signs each**
- Surya says "I take midday"; the Moon says "America is at midnight — I rule over the night." They tussle: the sun chases his property around the globe, but "no matter what, the sun can only own half the property."
- In Jyotish: wherever the sun is sitting, six signs from him belong to the sun. Sun sits in Aries, so Aries–Virgo (six signs) are his domain — called the day (diva).
- Libra–Pisces will be the night — the domain of the Moon. Bhuloka is equally divided: half day, half night.

**23.2 Sun travels direct, Moon travels retrograde; the Gandanta wall**
- The natural tendency of the Sun is direct: from Aries he reaches Virgo by going directly through Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Leo.
- The Moon sits in Pisces and wants to go directly to Libra but cannot — "the fire of the sun will boil him." He tries to go forward and hits a wall between Pisces and Aries, called Gandanta, and his body begins to be destroyed.

**23.3 Chandrashekhara — Shiva saves the Moon; the fishes' curse**
- As the Moon god prayed to be saved, God appeared in the form of a rishi not wearing much clothes, wearing the bark of a tree, holding a trident. The Moon was perplexed — expecting gods in gold with a crown — but this god said "Gold has no meaning for me. You are dying; I will give you life." He lifted the Moon god and put him on his head — from that day he became Chandrasekhara.
- "The God who can give me life is my God." The Moon realized all the money and wealth he ran after (largely because of wife Rohini, and Tara) was useless.
- The fishes suffer from the curse of the sun god: the sun cursed them as the cause of the Moon's birth (born in Pisces, the house of fishes), because half his domain got taken over. "Even today you take the fishes out of the water and put them on the land — they die."

**23.4 Sun lords the land, Moon lords the waters**
- The sun says: "I shall lord over the lands; you lord over the waters." Wherever there is land the sun lords over it; wherever there is water on the surface the Moon lords over it. "So long as there is land, human beings can live."

**23.5 First principle of Parashara — sabhya and apasavya; hora lagna**
- From Aries the rashis are counted directly (sabhya chakra / direct counting); from Pisces everything moves in the reverse direction (apasavya chakra / reverse counting). These two chakras exist together all the time.
- This is the "first principle of Parashara": from Aries, direct counting (because of the Sun); from Pisces, reverse counting (because of the Moon).
- Taught in hora lagna calculation: if your lagna is an odd sign, count from Aries to the odd sign; if the lagna is an even sign, come from Pisces in the reverse direction to the even sign.

## 24. Worship, Mantras & the Mind

**24.1 Reckoning signs; masculine/feminine; Saraswati as the first female**
- Aries and Pisces become the reckoning signs — the seeds of the Sun and the Moon, joined by the season Vasanta (spring). Though the Sun and Moon each move six signs, the Sun lords over the odd signs and the Moon over the even signs, because even signs are feminine and carry that nature from Pisces.
- Aries is the first masculine sign (Mesha); Pisces (Meena) is the first feminine sign. "If the first female is Meena, then that must be our Shaktarupa, our Saraswati — she was the first female."

**24.2 Only Saraswati reaches the top of the head; Soma mandala**
- Until then everybody was born from the mind of Brahma (manasaputras); all the rishis are born from his mind, from his head, called the Soma mandala (neck upwards).
- "Only Saraswati can reach to the top of the head; none of the other nadis can." Ganga (Ida) and Yamuna (Pingala) terminate at the Agya chakra; that chakra is the last Triveni — where the three rivers meet. Beyond it there is no more meeting; from there only one river continues, Sushumna, called Saraswati.

**24.3 Worshipping the Sun — one syllable or a 24-syllable mantra; the Gayatri**
- The Sun says: "I am the father of all, and your father is a representative of Surya. The first one to be worshipped is the Sun."
- The lagna is the root of the Sun; you worship him with one sound (bija, "Om" — the only way to reach the lagna) or with exactly 24 sounds (Sudarshana Chakra mathematics: 13 goes to the 2nd house, not the lagna; the lagna comes in year 1 and again in year 24).
- The Gayatri mantra as normally recited (Tat Savitur Varenyam ...) is 23 syllables and "will not go to the lagna" — it must be stretched (varanyam) to make it exactly 24 syllables.
- "Whether it is you or me, the sun created us, starting with the bija (the seed with which I was created) and ending with the Gayatri (my manifestation). You are the Gayatri, I am the Gayatri." When the dark ages of India came, the Brahmins hid the Gayatri — "only the high-class Brahmins are allowed to do it" — the politics of knowledge. But you are created by the sun god whether you are in America, Netherlands, or India.

**24.4 The Moon and the 12th house — dvadasakshari; the mind**
- The Moon is created in the 12th house, and the only way to reach that place is through a 12-syllable mantra, the dvadasakshari.
- Moon is the mind. "The mind is permanently tormented by tamas" — thoughts which are untruth (Saturn is untruth: "he took away my marble" — the marble never belonged to you; a 15-year-old does not play with marbles). "Go to the roots for everything, and you will solve your problems of life."

**24.5 Give the mind to Krishna — Om Namo Bhagavate Vasudevaya**
- In Bhagavad Gita, Krishna tells Arjuna: "Give the reins of your mind to me." The teacher's answer: "Om Namo Bhagavate Vasudevaya — the mind is now under your control; take over my houses, lead them where you think is right."
- The avatar is the sustainer of your life — he is sitting on the throne of time, and life is a period of time; "why not give the reins of your mind to him?"
- Never give the reins of your mind to a living person (who themselves has his own karmas) unless he is the avatar. The mind can be given only to your direct guru or to the avatar. Many gurus do nasty things and are "not free from the rajas of money" — until they become free, you cannot give them your mind.

**24.6 Choosing the avatar and the nakshatra lord method**
- Use a dvadasakshari mantra with an avatar of your liking: "Who should take over the reins of my mind?" Or choose based on the Moon — the lord of your moon sign (Shubhapati), or on the basis of the lordship of your nakshatra using Vimshottari dasha.
- Vedavyasa's signature is "Om Namo Bhagavate Vasudevaya": "I give my mind to Krishna; take over my reins; guide me; may your sattva always guide."
- Give the mind to Krishna and he will take you to the finest Shiva temples "in the right time doing the right puja" — you will be there on Shivaratri when the finest pandits come out to help you; the same for Durga puja. "Give your mind to the avatar; you choose the avatar. The way he will do it, nobody can."

**24.7 Entry is Surya, exit is Chandra**
- Birth comes from the lagna; the Sun decides where and how you are born. The 12th house decides death; "Sun may have given birth to you, but the Moon gets to decide when you will die."
- The Moon is the giver of longevity. "The moment you die, a fresh horoscope is made �?" that is birth in the other world... new horoscope is made, called Purniachary (the death/afterlife chart), and the one who is helping you to exit from this world is Chandra." Conclusion: "Entry is Surya, exit is Chandra."

---

# Astro Knowledge Base — Chaya, Upachaya, Trika & Marana Karaka Sthana (Lecture Transcript)

## 25. Chaya — what changes vs what stays

**25.1 Rashi vs Chaya**
- A rashi (sign) is the record of what is (total), while chaya is the incremental change — the increase or decrease of that record. Example: $100 is the rashi; if it goes to $108, +$8 is the chaya; if it drops to $92, −$8 is the chaya. Rashi is the astral record, chaya is how the record moves day by day.
- "The rashi is the average; the chaya is how much it grows or falls on a given day. Whichever planet gives you the chaya is the planet that produces your current situation."

**25.2 Rashi as the astral record of a soul**
- Each sign = 30 degrees; each degree ≈ 72 years of the soul's deeds. So one sign records about 2,160 years of a soul's astral track record (ancestral/previous family trait). Rashi is literally the total store: "the record of the positive and negative of all the 210 subjects studied earlier."
- Ayanamsha is the rate at which this record gets written — a Kala/rate factor. "The Ayanamsha is the rate of recording; every soul has its own record."

**25.3 Dirgha rashi and Chitra nakshatra mystery**
- Dirgha = long/deep signs. Sagittarius and Pisces are the longest (2h40m ascension). Virgo and Libra are the shortest (1h20m). So "sees less sky" = Virgo/Libra; the rest see more sky, i.e., more of the ecliptic.
- Chitra nakshatra sits at Virgo-Libra and is called the "shining jewel mind" — the boundary where the rashi flips to chaya; the mystery of how the mind records.

**25.4 Hora Gandanta — the losing of innocence**
- The Virgo/Libra gandanta (Hora Gandanta) is called "gandanta of innocence": at the Libra new moon the soul slips from Brahmacharya (Virgo) into Vivaha/marriage (Libra). "The moment you touch Libran energy, you lose your innocence."
- Whether the soul actually loses innocence depends on planets in the 7th house of the chart. Virgo gandanta is "gandanta of innocence"; the 12th house is "gandanta of moksha", the 30 degree point of Scorpio.

**25.5 Sri Ram example — Mangal in the 7th**
- Sri Ram has Mars (Mangal) in the 7th house (Libra) with the 8th lord there too. The "Librans desire" is symbolized by the bow that sits in the 7th house — Shiva's bow — waiting to be wielded. Janak keeps the bow, because Librans desire things that should be given away.
- "When Mars is in Libra, you fight for what should be given but is held by someone" — Ram broke the bow that was already his in a way. Conclusion: Mangal + Libra = "a warrior born of that bridge" (Rama was of Suryavamsha, born at the Saturn/Mars exact degree).

## 26. Upachaya and Apochaya — houses of increase and decrease

**26.1 Upachaya vs Apochaya meaning**
- Upachaya = increase; Apochaya = decrease. Upachaya houses from the lagna: 3, 6, 10, 11. The other 8 houses are apochaya (decreasing) houses. "If something must grow in your life, it works from the upachaya houses."
- House 2 and 12 are especially apochaya in nature.

**26.2 Bhavas are dynamic, not static**
- A bhava (house) is dynamic — the meaning shifts every day because the lagna moves ~1 degree a day. The one bhava that decides a lifetime (the borrowed aroodha from the lagna) is fixed for the 6 samhita birthdays.
- The houses are experienced through a fixed frame only for the ones that are relatively fixed: 2, 5, 8, 11. Every other house has a moving agenda day to day — "houses talk differently every day; only 2, 5, 8 and 11 are stable."

**26.3 Upachaya = lagna lord gives you fame**
- The upachaya houses (3, 6, 10, 11) are where energy increases. If the lagna lord (the spiritual self/soul-to-body binding) gets placed in an upachaya house, it uses that energy to give fame.
- Examples: Zulfiqar Ali Bhutto (Gemini lagna, 3rd house)... the lagna lord placed in the upachaya of a leader positions the person for struggle and eventual fame.

**26.4 The 6th house warning**
- The 6th house is an upachaya, but be careful: "with 6th house placements the enemy has the upper hand; he defeats you". It is the house of disease and enemies, so growth there is adversarial growth. The 7th is the house of open enemies, 8th of hidden enemies — these three (6, 7, 8) are the "warning" houses.

## 27. Derivation of the upachaya houses

**27.1 Shri, Ri and Kri — satva, rajas and tamas**
- Sankhya yukti: satva = Shri (remains steady — east), rajas = Ri (increase — south), tamas = Kri (decline — west). Sattwa houses: 4, 5, 9, 12; Rajas houses: 2, 3, 7, 10; Tamas houses: 1, 6, 8, 11.
- Sh, R, and K are the three roots: Shri, Ri, Kri. "Any quality of the planet is derived as a letter from the three roots Sh (sattva), R (rajas) and K (tamas)."

**27.2 From the 7th house these become 3, 6, 10, 11**
- Count satwa houses from the 7th (rather than the 1st): the 7th's 4 houses (10, 11, 2, 5 from lagna) shift to give the upachaya 3, 6, 10, 11. "From the 7th the parswe... the 3, 6, 10 and 11 come out" — the sattwa houses as seen from the 7th = the upachaya houses from the lagna.
- So upachaya = "where the sattva of the partner (7th) grows you."

**27.3 Money growth path — 11 vs 3**
- The 11th house is where money grows (income); the 12th is where money falls (expenditure); the 4th is where happiness grows. "Give the 4th house the limit of a sadhu so that desire never ruins your finances."
- The 3rd, 6th, 10th and 11th are the growth houses of the second half of the chart where the money cycle (2nd = accumulated wealth) expands.

**27.4 7th house money and Mangal in the 7th**
- A malefic in the 7th destroys money; a benefic in the 7th increases money. This is why Mars in the 7th is difficult for partnerships and finances.
- But the same Mars in the 7th with the 8th lord in Libra (as in Sri Ram) makes the person a fighter for the "given but withheld" — the warrior who restores balance.

## 28. Trika — the three ka houses (3, 6, 11)

**28.1 What is Trika?**
- Trika = the three ka houses: 3rd, 6th and 11th. "Ka" is the name of the devata of Shani (Saturn), and ka = Prajapati, the creator. Saturn's devata is Prajapati Brahma. the letter ka is the seed of both Shani and Brahma.
- The three ka houses are where the body copy is created (ka = creating a copy of the model/template).

**28.2 The body is built through the trika**
- The 3rd house = the chest/birth; the 6th = the seat of creation (Marana Karaka Sthana, the death-to-life station); the 11th = delivery. So conception-to-birth runs through the trika. The genetic code template (the model) is Brahma's; the body copy is Shani's.
- "Brahma = the genetic template; Shani literally copies the model into a body. That is the trika — the three stations of creating your material body."

**28.3 Derivation of the Kali mantra**
- Ka + ka + ka (the three ka's, three houses) + a = kra; ring = the sound of Kali's energization. So the Kring mantra of Kali is derived from the trika of Ka (Shani/Prajapati). "Kali's vidya = the science of the trika — the knowledge of how the body is born and dies."
- Ka (Saturn) → the planet Shani is the giver of the body you acquire in each birth cycle; Kali = Shani's teacher of endings.

**28.4 Brahma's seat is the 6th house; Saraswati in Pisces**
- The 6th house (Virgo on the zodiac) is the "day sign" of Rahu and the seat of Brahma — the creator's workshop. Bhadrapada is the month (Aug-Sep) associated with Brahma's bed in the 6th.
- Saraswati resides in Pisces (12th on the zodiac); the creator's wife' Knowledge stays in the invisible — 12th from the 6th = the 11th, hence the 11th house delivers what the 6th creates.

**28.5 Saraswati Puja — when the mind is near the moon**
- Saraswati Puja is done when the Moon is in Pisces... the tool the mind uses is memory (Saraswati). "The date of worship follows the moon, because Saraswati is memory and memory is recorded by the Moon."
- Panchami (5th tithi) is the day of Saraswati Puja; the 5th house is the house of the mind's creativity, so the tithi that rules the mind is puja day.

## 29. Who builds the body and the mind — the Saptarishis

**29.1 The seven rishis build the body (hardware)**
- Seven rishis literally build the physical body — 7 body parts: Surya → Kashyap rishi → bones; Attri → blood and heart; Mangal (Angira) → nerves, brain and spine; Shani → muscles; Shukra → the sex organ; Mercury → the skin. The body = the hardware assembled by the seven rishis.
- Shani's muscle is the motor; Shukra's organ is the reproduction; Mercury's skin is the surface of the machine.

**29.2 The mind is the software, born in the 12th**
- The mind is "software" and it is born in the 12th house. Ten rishis make up the mind: 10 rishis + 10 devatas = the mind's 10 (the 10 directions, the 10 sense-and-motor pairs). "The mind is formed by 10 rishis and awakened by 10 devatas — the software."
- At the 3rd month of pregnancy the brain functions begin; the 3rd house is linked to the birth of the chest/mind's instrument. So the "ka" of the 3rd is the mind starting to record.

**29.3 The three rishis apart from the body**
- Of the original group, three rishis are not part of the body-building (the mind's software has its own three). The body has 7 rishis, the mind has 10 — together they account for the full set, minus three who remain as the witness/seers. "Three rishis watch; they are not in the creation."

## 30. Applications — Abraham Lincoln and rebirth desire

**30.1 Abraham Lincoln — Aquarius lagna, Ketu in the 3rd**
- Lincoln: Aquarius lagna, Ketu (freedom, no-colors) transiting/exalted... Ketu in the 3rd house. The 3rd is a trika (ka) house — the desire to free. Ketu there = "desire to free": Lincoln's soul-desire was abolition — freeing slaves.
- "The 3rd house from the lagna with Ketu = the desire for freedom; the man whose life's work is to cut the rope (the 3rd = the rope that binds the neck)."

**30.2 Rebirth desire sends you to the 12th and re-birth**
- The desire of the dead (the 12th) is the seed of the next birth; the Moon decides death (12th), the Sun decides birth (lagna). Upachaya showing = the direction in which the soul wants to GROW in the next life. "Saturn's ka houses tell you the growth the next body is built for."

## 31. Marana Karaka Sthana (MKS) — where a planet is killed

**31.1 The nine MKS positions**
- The Marana Karaka Sthana (house where a planet gets killed / made dead): Sun → 12th; Moon → 8th; Mars → 7th; Mercury → 4th and 7th; Jupiter → 3rd; Venus → 6th; Saturn → the lagna (1st); Rahu → 9th; Ketu → 4th.
- Once a planet sits in its MKS, "the planet is dead for the victim tribe — the tribe signifying that house gets destroyed."

**31.2 MKS for the zodiac tribes**
- When the Sun is in the 12th, the victim is the sign Leo (Sun's rashi) — the devastating "Maryada Purushottam death". Each MKS lists the sign that gets killed: Sun-12th kills the Leo tribe; Moon-8th kills the Cancer tribe; Mars-7th kills Leo; Mercury-4th/7th kills Virgo; Jupiter-3rd kills Sagittarius and Pisces; Venus-6th kills Libra; Saturn-1st kills Capricorn, Aquarius; Rahu-9th kills Aquarius (the rashi); Ketu-4th kills Cancer.
- "These are the exact viraha (separation) positions — the ones who suffer in the tribe are the native and the natives' family line."

**31.3 MKS vs debility — the curse is worse**
- Two types of killing: the MKS (house) and the debility (rasi). The MKS may be worse: "a debilitated planet carries a curse; an MKS planet carries the victim's dead — the tribe's destruction." Both are powerful; MKS is often more violent.
- MKS evaluation happens from the dasha rashi (the rashi of the running dasha), not only from the lagna.

**31.4 Velupillai Prabhakaran — the MKS example**
- Prabhakaran: Aries lagna (possibly Leo lagna variant in the charts cited)... Rahu and MKS placements of a Taurus lagna... the "tribe destruction" example: with an MKS Mars and the Sun in its 12th, the person's tribe (Tamil Eelam movement) is destroyed.
- Also Zulfiqar Ali Bhutto (Gemini lagna) with lagna lord in the 3rd (trika) — his life's career was struggle (3rd) and his fame came from the upachaya working — he was hanged eventually. The 6th house warning: "leaders with strong upachaya must watch the 6th — the enemy gets the upper hand."

# Astro Knowledge Base — Aachhadana Series: Chandra, Mangala, Buddha and Guru (Lecture Transcript)

## 32. Chandra Aachhadana and Grahana Yoga

**32.1 Counting in reverse when Ketu is involved**
- Whenever you count houses from a planet conjoined with Ketu, the counting goes in the reverse direction. "Ketu has the power to reverse everything. He is fully retrograde. Rahu does not have this power."
- Example: Moon in Cancer with Ketu — ninth house from the Moon by normal counting would be Aquarius, but counting in reverse it is Scorpio. Whenever Ketu is with a planet, its aachhadana counting must be reversed.

**32.2 Rahu is a bhogi; Ketu is not**
- Rahu enjoys the bhogas (enjoyments) of the planet it conjoins; Ketu does not. When counting houses: from Rahu you count forward, from Ketu you count backward. Ketu's interest houses are counted backwards (2nd from Ketu = Shani, 3rd = Mesha, 4th = Mangal in Mina, 5th = Shukra...); Rahu counts forward (2nd = Shukra, 3rd = Mangal, 5th = Shani...).
- Rahu and Ketu together cover only half the zodiac with their interest houses; the other half is outside their purview — "from here they start fighting each other."

**32.3 Grahana Yoga (eclipse)**
- Moon + Ketu and Sun + Rahu are Grahana (eclipse) yogas. If a horoscope has these combinations, the eclipse effect will happen; whenever it hits, it causes a lot of disturbance in the house where the combination sits.
- Moon + Ketu makes the mind very peculiar and extreme. There is a dharma conflict: "the peaceful moon tells you follow this path, the agitated Ketu tells no, something else" — extremism and dharma confusion.

**32.4 Remedy for Ketu-Grahana of the Moon**
- If Ketu is doing grahana of the Moon, the remedy is Ganesh worship and fasting — Sankastha Chaturthi (one of the two Chaturthi fasts: Sankasta and Vinayaka). Worshiping Ganapati and keeping the vratas removes the Chandra dosha.

**32.5 Donald Trump's fourth house Ketu-Moon**
- US President Donald Trump has the Ketu + Moon combination in the fourth house — a Grahana Yoga. The fourth house is home, so "the home is in trouble" — the disturbance hits the home. When Ketu loses its exaltation, things will calm down completely.

## 33. The Casablanca Bomber example

**33.1 Chandra Aachhadana and the trines**
- The aachhadana from Chandra (reverse counting as needed) shows dharma and what makes the mind happy. The trines from the aachhadana hold the three generations: grandfather in the 9th, the native in the 1st, the child in the 5th. "The aachhadana becomes the father."
- From Chandra aachhadana you see "where is the mind, what does the mind want to do, what is making the mind happy."

**33.2 Pisacha Yoga (Mars + Ketu)**
- The combination of Mars + Ketu is called Pisacha Yoga and it generates tremendous fear. "Everybody is afraid of something. This yoga generates that fear." The native does something by which he wants to terrorize the world.

**33.3 The Casablanca Bomber's dharma conflict**
- Case: Cancer lagna, Moon + Ketu (Grahana Yoga), Chandra aachhadana ninth with Mars + Ketu (Pisacha Yoga) — father/religion point in Scorpio. The peaceful moon says one dharma, Ketu says another: dharma conflict and extremism.
- He converted to Islam in his 18th year (in Turkey) and was indoctrinated in Afghanistan. The 18th year (13-24 counted from the rashi after the lagna) falls in the rashi whose lord is conjoined with another Grahana Yoga (Sun + Rahu). He became the Casablanca Bomber.

## 34. Mangala Aachhadana — strength from Mars

**34.1 Mars is the strength of the Kalapurusha**
- Mars is the strength of the Kalapurusha: "this is my strength, the gift of Mangal." The house Mars sits in is where you have power, position, status and authority; people will listen to you or fear you. Mars is the governor of Bhuloka.
- Worshiping Hanuman or Kartikeya enlivens that house. The 11th from Mars (Mangala Labha) shows the "red trident" — the gift of nature, of the sky-father, of Shiva; labha means to obtain something, a benefit, deep understanding.

**34.2 The 11th from Mars is strong; the 12th is destroyed**
- Any planet in the 11th from Mars gets strength from Mangal. Any planet in the 12th from Mars is destroyed by Mangal. "A planet in the 12th house is surely going to get destroyed."
- Houses from Mars can be good, bad, evil or dead. Mars in the Marana Karaka Sthana (7th) is a dead end for enemies, but also for servants/workers (e.g. a factory fire kills many).

**34.3 Mars digbala caution and Ekadasha Rudra**
- Even Mars in the 10th with digbala: because Mars is papagra/tamas, when tamas becomes very strong "you will sit at home and play music — no work." Mars in the 10th = fire in the 10th = your work is finished; it also gives an 8th of retirement (you keep getting retired).
- Mars in the 11th is called Ekadasha Rudra — the power to protect. Mars protects the sign of its 11th and its planets. Rahu-Mars: Rahu first does Kuja Stambhana (puts out the fire), then Mangal gets angry and takes revenge — "nobody can take revenge better than Mangal." The Rahu-Mars conjunction ends up destroying Rahu.

**34.4 Lord Krishna — Mars in the third**
- Krishna: Mars in the 3rd, the 11th from it is the lagna, so the lagna and the Moon become super powerful. The 11th's lord is Venus, so Mars promises to protect Taurus and the planets there — "Mangal always keeps his promise." He destroys all the demons so Rahu cannot do Kuja Stambhana; instead Rahu gets destroyed.

**34.5 Sri Ram — Mars in the 7th and the Lanka**
- Sri Ram has Mars in the 7th; Ravana's drishti fell on Mars and on the 7th — so he kidnapped Sita. Mars (having Rahu in the 12th from it) went to Lanka and set it on fire — "Mangal likes to play with fire." End result: Rahu got destroyed because it was in the 12th from Mars.

## 35. Mangala Aachhadana applied to warriors and geniuses

**35.1 Great boxers — Muhammad Ali and Rocky Marciano**
- Muhammad Ali: Moon sign Makara, Saturn shubhapati; Mars aachhadana 11th holds Ketu, and the lord of that sign (Saturn) sits in the 10th. Strength came in Saturn dasha (career roughly 1960-1981, Dwisaptati dasha because lagna lord is in the 7th). He lost and regained titles because of a retrograde (loss and gain).
- Rocky Marciano: undefeated champion. His 11th-from-Mars lord is Mercury sitting with Saturn (muscle). Whenever Saturn (muscle) gets the strength of Mars, boxing is very good.

**35.2 Great generals — Rommel and Patton**
- Rommel: Mars + Saturn in the 10th (Yama); 11th lord is the Moon in Mesha aspected by Mars — a strong Moon-Mars mind that is very difficult to defeat. The Moon has rashi drishti on planets in the 12th — great strategy in foreign lands.
- George Patton: Mars in the 4th (perfect); 11th from it is Saturn's sign, and the 11th lord Mercury is in Mesha (house of Mars) in the 7th of war with Mars' graha drishti — "I want you to sacrifice the enemy soldiers for my country." Rommel was afraid only of Patton.
- Rule: "If the 11th lord from Mangal is in Mesha or Vrischika and aspected by Mangal, it is a very deadly general — don't fight with him."
- Surya Maranakaraka: when Surya is in its MKS (12th), the boss is a bigger problem than the father. Rommel's boss was Hitler (Surya MKS); he tried to assassinate Hitler and was killed.

**35.3 Chess grandmasters**
- Chess signature: the 11th lord from Mars associated with both Mangal and Rahu (Mars = nerves of steel, Rahu = strategy). Mars is strongest in Capricorn (exalted) and Gemini (11th lord is Mars itself).
- Bobby Fischer: Mars in the 7th (MKS) with mutual rashi drishti with Rahu. The 11th lord is Mars itself (Scorpio aachhadana) and the other 11th lord Ketu joins Mercury and Surya, with Surya-Rahu parivartana. He stopped playing in 1972 (Moon dasha, Mars antardasha) and restarted in Rahu-Jupiter. He fought the U.S. government over Yugoslavia, went into hiding, was detained in Japan, and died in Iceland in 2008. Too strong, no fear of anyone.
- Kasparov: Mars in the 9th, 11th from it is the 7th; lord Venus gets Mars' graha drishti and Rahu's rashi drishti — nerves of steel + strategy, nothing else aspects it.
- Anand: 11th from Mars holds Moon and Mercury (good temperament); the lord Jupiter receives Mars' and Rahu's rashi drishti. "In both cases Rahu and Mangal are there — that is the straight shot for chess."

## 36. Buddha Aachhadana — Mercury

**36.1 Mercury is the body and the skin**
- Mercury rules Prithvi tatva (solid substance) and gives the body its looks and skin. "When you look at a person you are only looking at his skin. Beauty is only skin deep." Buddha is sharira; the first house from Mercury shows what kind of body.
- The skin has seven layers — seven layers of protection. As long as the skin is unharmed, "I am unharmed."

**36.2 Learning and speech from Mercury**
- Mercury shows speech, learning, education and the means of earning wealth. An educated person talks differently; from the way a person talks you can judge the Mercury in their horoscope. Planets conjoined with Mercury modify the speech: benefics = positive talk, malefics = negative talk. Exalted/Vargottama Mercury = excited, powerful speech.
- Speech shows class and community, friendships, and communication (all communication is from Mercury); melody comes from the Moon.

**36.3 Mercury afflictions and remedies**
- Mercury + Mars: speech sounds strict, like giving orders; people think you are harsh. Remedy: field sports / jogging — "sweat from the skin" (sweat is salty) removes the Mangal-Mercury problem.
- Mercury alone with papa kartari means you live alone with very few friends. Lagna with papa kartari from Surya and Mangal can damage the physical body.
- Mercury's deha maraka is the 7th house. Mercury in the 3rd gives danger at childbirth for a lady; in the 11th, danger to a man at childbirth (due to asoucha/cleanness impurity). Moon + Venus are very negative for Mars.

**36.4 High-class speech and the Gandhi-Nehru bond**
- An exalted Moon or Mercury in Rohini gives very high-class speech (the "Rohini club"). Mercury + Venus in Tula gives sweet speech with emotional power — matters of love are expressed easily and nicely; people may wrongly think a person doesn't love if Venus is not associated with Mercury.
- Gandhi: Mercury + Venus (sweet emotion) + Mars (bitter, strict, "no means no") in Tula with Moon in Cancer. Nehru similar. Because both have Mercury and Venus in Tula and Moon in Cancer, the partnership worked 100% — similar planets give a perfect friendship/partnership (for partnership; marriage is separate).

**36.5 Vak differs in varga**
- The same person speaks differently with different people: rashi chart for the general speech/dharma, and divisional charts for speech with different people (brothers, children, students, parents, spouse). From divisional charts you can see the "speech" of the person in each relationship. The body is judged only from the rashi chart.

## 37. Guru Aachhadana — Jupiter

**37.1 The third from Jupiter is the hand of Shiva**
- The aachhadana of Jupiter is the third house from Jupiter — the "hand of God." The 3rd is the right hand, the 11th is the left hand (for Mars we use the left hand, for Guru the right hand). This is the real trishul, the spiritual trident of Shiva (Shankara).
- Jupiter's upadesha (teaching) gives the right gati (path) and overcomes the strife of Kali Yuga. Jupiter empowers one planet, and that empowered planet shows miraculous results. Doing a mantra associated with the acharya (Jupiter) makes Jupiter happy — "you are obeying the guru, listening to the guru, not your own wish."

**37.2 The spiritual path trigger**
- Any person (elderly/guru) associated with the third-from-Jupiter house can trigger your spiritual path — "it becomes the beginning of a great journey even without your realizing it; after 20-30 years you thank that person from your soul."
- If the bhagya karaka / the karaka of divine grace is in the third house or connected with it, the trigger is 100% sure. If the atma karaka is associated, it is a very strong guru kripa.

**37.3 Sannyasa comes when the hand of Shiva is lifted**
- Sannyasa comes when the right hand of Shiva is lifted (the trident in the third from Jupiter). "So long as Lord Shiva is not lifting his hand, you will not get sannyasa, no matter how big a shot you are."
- Shankaracharyas: Sri Abhinavatirtha (Shringeri) — Jupiter in Taurus, third = Cancer; sannyasa 22 May 1931 in Rahu-Moon (Moon is lord of Cancer); became head 16 Oct 1954 in Saturn-Mercury. Sri Chandrasekharendra/Vijendra Saraswati (Kanchi) — Jupiter in the 1st with Ketu (reverse counting) in Cancer; sannyasa 29 May 1983 in Moon-Sun. Jayendra Saraswati — Jupiter in Karka, third with Rahu (nodes confirm Shankara sannyasa); sannyasa in Rahu-Mars; became Pithadipati in Mercury-Venus.
- For sannyasa, parivartana does not work (sannyasa is like death); use the permanent rashi drishti. Parivartana works for the administrative Pithadipati role.

**37.4 Rishi Linga — Ketu in the third from Jupiter**
- If Ketu is in the third from Jupiter it is a Rishi Linga — you are guided directly by a rishi. Based on the planet in the sign (or its lord) you decide the rishi: Ketu = Narada (absolute detachment), Mercury = Pulastya/Agastya, Sun = Kashyapa, Rahu = someone from the era (e.g. Roma Harshan of Veda Vyasa's time).
- Maharshi Mahesh Yogi: Jupiter's third holds Ketu (Rishi Linga of the Jyotir Mat); brahmachari in 1943 (Mercury antardasha — Mercury is the lord of the sign); left Uttarakhand to teach in 1955.

**37.5 Ramana Maharshi — the Agni Linga**
- Ramana Maharshi: Jupiter in Aquarius → third is Aries holding Mars (Agni tatva): the deity is the Agni Linga at Arunachala. On 16 Nov 1895 (age 16) in Saturn-Venus he realized Arunachala; in Saturn-Sun he had a powerful Kundalini experience.
- With Sun + Rahu (Grahana Yoga) look at Jupiter for the results — Dakshinamurti (Omkara) knowledge awakens. Only Atri has the Turiya knowledge of Omkara; Turiya Omkara prevents death — "where there is Omkara, death will not happen."

**37.6 Other guru charts**
- Abhishiktananda (French): Jupiter in Kanya, third holds Ketu (Rishi Linga); established Sachinanda Ashram in Mercury-Venus (Venus in Karka has rashi drishti); became Guru Gyananand Giri in Mercury-Rahu (Rahu = Atma Karaka); left for the Himalayas in Venus dasha.
- Hazrat Inayat Khan: Jupiter + Ketu (reverse counting) → third is Pisces; Sufism is associated with Pisces (universal like the ocean — God is for everybody). Jupiter and Ketu conjoin Saturn; Pisces lagna with Moon in the 12th made him the great Sufi musician (published 75 musicals).
- Swami Vivekananda: Jupiter in Libra → third is the lagna holding the Sun (his Atma Karaka) — the Atma Linga; the rishi is Kashyapa. Ramakrishna said without computation "this is Rishi Kashyapa who has come." Saturn (his karaka) with the Moon has rashi drishti on the third — giving the powerful Kali diksha: "this Kali diksha is so powerful that she will not leave you wherever you go."
- Aga Khan III: Jupiter in the 8th (very wealthy); third = Aquarius holding Rahu (Atma Karaka) with Mars and Saturn; in 1885 in Moon-Sun (Sun has rashi drishti) he was elevated as the 48th Nizari Iman of Shia. Use permanent rashi drishti for life changes; graha (desire) drishti is temporary.

# Astro Knowledge Base — Agni, the Tara Grahas, and Shani (Lecture Transcript)

## 38. Mangala — Agni and heat

**38.1 Mars has a red color and rules heat**
- Mars seen in the night sky has a reddish light; this color has been identified with Agni, and Mars is the controller/governor of Agni — he tells how Agni is utilized. Mars particularly rules heat in the system.
- The Sun produces two things: heat and light. Light removes darkness and is the greatest blessing; all life goes toward light (even plants tilt toward a window). Heat heats the atmosphere and the body; hot body = alive body, cold body = dead body. When body temperature rises, the heat in the body is fighting the disease. "Heat is the fighter." Mars is symbolized by a red triangle.

**38.2 Fire is bad in water signs**
- Cancer, Scorpio and Pisces are the natural water signs. Fire is very bad in water — water puts the fire out, the fire sizzles out, causing disease and destruction in the body.
- So Mars in the 4th (imitating Cancer/Karka water), 8th (imitating Scorpio water) or 12th (imitating Pisces water) is destroyed by jala. Wherever the heat is destroyed, something in life will suddenly die out (like a matchstick put in a glass of water) — "you may be alive but something will die in your life." This is like a Gandanta (fire-water) experience repeated again and again.

**38.3 Kuja dosha**
- Kuja = bad (ku) + birth (ja). Mangal in the 1st house is Kuja dosha — a bad birth; the mother suffered greatly (knives, painful surgery) at birth. Mars associating with the lagna: in rashi it is a bad childhood; in navamsa it is a bad delivery/danger to the mother. "So Mars in the lagna is really Kuja dosha."
- Mars in the 7th is also Kuja dosha: coming (birth) is painful and going (death) is painful — "the coming is painful and the going is painfully severe." As a result Kuja dosha applies to the 1st and 7th houses when Mars is there.

**38.4 Sri Ram's Mars in the seventh and the Gayatri**
- The 7th house is called Astha Lagna (sunset/death point, or before that marriage). Sri Ram had Mars in the 7th. Vishwamitra came and took Rama at 16 — Vishwamitra is the giver of the Gayatri mantra, so "Gayatri has come to Rama." Gayatri is the mantra of Agni Devata. The Gayatri fully energized Ram's Mars (the manushya/Agni who could kill Ravana) — the bow of Shiva broke and he married Sita.
- Gayatri mantras: 24-syllable Gayatri (Tatsaviturvaranyam...) is for Surya; 28-syllable (Om Bhur Bhuva Swa...) is for Mars/Mangala. The remedy for Mars is Gayatri. Without proper initiation do not do the Mars mantra — "do not play with the fire of Mars." A proper Gayatri initiation takes 3-7 days. Once recited, Mangal will awaken and the fire will come.

**38.5 Five bad places for Agni**
- The five horrible placements for Agni planets: 1st, 4th, 7th, 8th and 12th houses. Lagna is the entry and the 7th/12th are the exits; "if there is fire in the exit you will get burnt while going out."
- Surya in the 4th/8th/12th is also extremely bad (bhava dosha). Ketu in the 4th: "I will destroy your house, burn it to smoke." Ketu in the 8th: a powerful kundalini experience whether you want it or not. Ketu in the 12th: "there is a snake sleeping in the bed with you."
- The 4th/8th/12th are bad regardless of lagna. Home is where the Sun and Moon come to sleep. Ketu in the 4th eclipses and destroys the Moon/the mother's happiness — Ketu in the 4th is the worst (it is a Marana Karaka Sthana). Mangal in the 7th is the worst; Surya in the 12th is the worst.
- Mars in the 12th = bed in fire; Mars in the 8th = spouse in fire (Patnika vyaagya); Mars in the 4th = house in fire. Mars is lord of Scorpio (a water sign) — hence the marriage problems. Q&A: if you want a very long-lived spouse, do not touch the spouse (e.g. Ramakrishna and Sharada Maa).

## 39. Shani Dev

**39.1 Manda — the slow mover and vata**
- Saturn is Shanaischara — slow moving; the word is Manda (moving slowly). Movement is vata (air). Slow air is good (a calm, nice breeze); a typhoon/hurricane creates a tsunami. "Thank God Shani is slow; his slow motion is what is good for the world."
- Shani has two sons: Manda (vata energy) destroys memory — wherever Manda is, the memory pertaining to that thing gets destroyed; and Mandi (born of Manda). If Manda is in the 7th, even your spouse's memory for you is destroyed.

**39.2 Shani's birth and tapasya**
- Surya as Vivasvan (in Virgo) produced three children: Manu, Tapati, and Shani. Shani (third son) attained the status of a graha by tremendous tapasya. "Nobody can do tapasya like Shani. You want to do penance? You need the help of Shani."
- Story: Brihaspati and Shukracharya both wanted the Mrityunjaya mantra from Shiva. The condition: hang upside down (retrograde) for 20 years and breathe smoke. Saturn = the tapasya/penance, Ketu = the smoke being breathed, Shukra = the one who completed the tapasya correctly and got the mantra. When these planets (Saturn + Ketu + Venus) are associated, the person is born in a yoga of tapasya (a tapasvi).

**39.3 Sadhana vs tapasya**
- Sadhana is for a siddhi — to gain an ability; tapasya is not for getting something but for removing something: to burn away tamas so the soul is released. Tapas means to burn; the body burns from inside, and sadhus suffer diseases because "tapas will burn and destroy the body." If you burn your body down, Mother Earth will not give you another body — no more birth, moksha.

**39.4 Nilavasa and Varuna**
- Shani loves to wear dark blue (Neela = dark/indigo blue, not light blue). The color is derived from the sea/ocean. Navy personnel all over the world do the mantra "Shamno Varuna" — "O Varuna (god of all waters), protect me." When Saturn is in the 7th house he is Varuna Deva (digbala). The dark blue color of sailors is Saturn's color.

**39.5 Shani worships Brahma; the 6th-7th-8th divine houses**
- Shani worships Brahma in the Brahma Sabha, which is in the nabhi (navel) — the Manipura chakra, mapping to Swargaloka; the chakra done for it is the Swarga/Dikpala chakra.
- Houses 6, 7 and 8 carry our experience of God from the womb. The 7th house is the Adhana Lagna (lagna of conception). In the mother's womb: the 6th (12th from the 7th) = the elders/ancestors, where Brahma's will is; the 7th = the conception given by Vishnu/Jagannath; the 8th = Rudra. Garbha vasam mahadukhat — "when you are in the womb it is the greatest sorrow; O Madhusudhana have mercy on me." There is no sorrow greater than the womb and no pain greater than delivery.
- Therefore: Brahma in the 6th, Vishnu in the 7th, Rudra in the 8th. Saturn's devata is Brahma, Jupiter's is Vishnu, Mars' is Rudra. Saturn in the 6th is fantastic — he is worshiping/praying to Brahma with the bija "Aham Brahmasmi" (know/realize yourself). Vishnu does not worship himself; Vishnu worships Rudra, Jupiter worships Shiva, Mars worships Vishnu. Examples: Hanuman (Rudra) worships Ram; Arjuna (Rudra) worships Krishna.

**39.6 Shani's dictums and the Dushasana-Draupadi-Bhima story**
- Shani is effulgent, intense, and serious — "don't try to crack a joke in front of him." A Shani dictum: "a woman who laughs too much is characterless; if women laugh too loudly they will lose their shame."
- Mahabharata: Dushasana visited Indraprastha, walked onto what looked like a carpet but was water, got wet; Draupadi laughed loudly at him. Dushasana was insulted and angry — "Shani had entered Dushasana" — and he vowed revenge. In the dice game, Draupadi was lost and Dushasana tried to disrobe her; Krishna protected her. Bhima (the third brother, Ketu — "the third brother always loses the head") vowed to pull out Dushasana's heart and drink his blood; Kali entered Bhima's consciousness (he worshiped the Kalika of Delhi daily) just as Shani entered Dushasana's. "Shani is the thought of revenge — it will destroy you more than anything else. Understand karma; don't allow Shani into your consciousness."
- The 6th house: asuras pray to Brahma through Shani (e.g. Ravana's boon that no Deva/Rakshasa/Kinnara/Gandharva could kill him — forgetting Manushya, so Rama was born a man).

**39.7 Tripurasura and Narakasura — Shani in battle**
- Tripurasura = Bhur-Bhuva-Swa (Bhuloka = 1st, Bhuvaloka = 5th, Swargaloka = 9th) taken over by demons. If demons (malefics: Mangal, Shani, Rahu, Ketu, Mandi, Gulika) dominate the 1st, 5th and 9th houses, your horoscope is taken over by Tripurasura — your shakti (1-5-9 triangle) is kidnapped by asuras. Remedy: Shiva sadhana. The only graha who will fight for you is Shani — Jupiter only meditates and all the grahas shiver and run away.
- Shani gets courage because he is the lord of Capricorn — "I am the lord of the graveyard, a ghost bigger than all the ghosts; I will make a graveyard for the asuras. That is Shani Mahakala."
- Narakasura drags all dying souls to hell (Yama loves it). Shani saves souls from going to Naraka: there are 7 talas ruled by Saturn and 28 narakas ruled by Mars. Shani fights Mars: "don't send them to Naraka; send them to the talas, I will make them labor and improve." Shani punishes here and now in Bhuloka instead of deferring to hell.
- In Brahma Muhurta, if you worship Shani, you will not go to Naraka. Shani's station is Mahadosha: wherever Shani is placed, that rashi and bhava become dirty; all disease comes from there. Chronic suffering: Shani in the 10th = career is chronic suffering; 9th = dharma; 7th = relationships; 4th = home/mother's sorrow. Shani's drishti is destructive; his conjunction destroys the karakatwa of the planet (Jupiter = children/divinity, 9th = dharma, 2nd = wealth; Saturn conjoining Jupiter can destroy your children/dharma; Saturn conjoining Venus loses the wife/marriage).

**39.8 Purpose of Jyotish and the Gayatri**
- First you learn good vs bad placement; then you realize good has some bad and bad has some good; then good and bad become emotional (what makes me happy vs sad). "Jyotish is Agni — fire always goes upwards." Agni inside you makes you rise after death, not go down to the talas or narakas. The mantra that guarantees a strong Agni is the Gayatri — the compressed total energy of Sri Ramachandra/Jagannath.

## 40. The luminaries — Sun and Moon in the body

**40.1 Pinda and Brahmanda; the eyes**
- The body is pinda and the universe is brahmanda; the whole brahmanda is inside the pinda and the pinda can expand into brahmanda. Saturn sits in the chest (breath) — he keeps you alive, so he is connected to longevity ("the power to breathe all the time is the blessing of Shani"). The five tara grahas live inside the body.
- The Sun is in the man's right eye and the Moon in the left; for a woman it is reversed. Men are created from the Sun (solar/masculine energies dominate), women from the Moon; men seek the moon (spouse), women seek the sun. Sun rules wealth, Moon rules health — men are better at money, women at health. Left brain = logic (Sun), right brain = creative (Moon); the right hand (Unnata/increase) is ruled by the Sun, the left hand (Natta/decrease) by the Moon — the Moon only reflects the Sun's light and therefore decreases.

**40.2 Lagna is the third eye; guru of day and night**
- Lagna is the third eye / the Brahmaandra — the soft spot on a newborn's head that closes within months. While the gate is open the devatas can talk with the baby (happy babies are visited by devatas).
- If born in the daytime, Jupiter is the guru karaka; if born at night, Venus is the guru karaka. This shows how well the connection between the third eye and the brain works. Daytime: check Jupiter (combust/hit/blocked → wrong decisions). Night time: check Venus (combust/hit/blocked → wrong decisions, self-blame, self-pity). Many astrologers fail to check the daytime/nighttime birth.
- Day birth + Jupiter yoga → naturally inclined to 24-syllable Gayatri Chanda mantras; night birth → 32-syllable Anushtup Chanda mantras. Jupiter likes Gayatri, Venus likes Anushtup.

**40.3 Sri Ram and Krishna — day and night examples**
- Sri Ram: born at midday, Cancer lagna, Jupiter exalted in the lagna — he understood every word the maharishis said (Jupiter in the lagna is more relevant for a day-born). Lord Krishna: born at midnight, Taurus lagna — Shukra (lagna lord) connected to the lagna with a Venus-Moon parivartana; a powerful Venus for a night-born. Native intelligence is seen from the Navamsa chart, not the rashi chart.

**40.4 Rahu and Ketu in the body; Dagda and the nakshatras**
- Rahu is in the head (pushing down like gravity above the neck) and Ketu is in the torso/lower body (the spine holding the 33 devatas; Ketu is associated with devatas, and Surya is the topmost deva, so Surya loves Ketu and pushes up). The neck has two pressures; when the spine is erect there is burning at the neck, but lying flat there is no pressure — Rahu-Ketu work with gravity when erect, so lying down is the natural relaxation.
- The luminaries are in the eyes and are constantly wary of Rahu and Ketu. Rahu eclipses the Sun through the rashi — in whichever rashi Rahu sits, the lord of that rashi carries Rahuian energy. If Rahu is in Mars' sign: secretly violent, greedy for property/power. In Saturn's sign: greedy for slaves/free labor. In its own signs: poisons/alcohol/cocaine. In Venus signs (Taurus/Libra): greed for the opposite sex — Bill Clinton had Rahu in Taurus. In Mercury signs: greedy for money/childish things. In Jupiter signs: greedy for people's lives/children.
- Ketu eclipses the Moon through the nakshatra — look at the nakshatra Ketu occupies; the eclipse hits your mind/life through that karakatwa (e.g. Ketu in a Venus nakshatra: your spouse becomes vicious like a snake who chases you mercilessly). Ketu as a graha in the Ketu/Vimshottari nakshatras — Ashwini (Bhu/health), Magha (Bhuva/wealth), Moola (Swarga/wisdom) — these are Gandanta nakshatras; any planet there suffers (e.g. Jupiter in Ashwini = health gone, in Magha = wealth gone, in Moola = wisdom gone). If the Moon (lagna lord) is in Magha you will lose your head; in Moola your wisdom.

**40.5 Dagda nakshatras and the snake story**
- Dagda means burnt/roasted. By its mere presence, a nakshatra gets burnt (Dagda nakshatra). Example: Surya in Ashlesha (ruled by the serpents). Parashara, 5000 years ago, performed a snake yajna where snakes jumped into the fire and burnt; only Vasuki escaped by renouncing and going to Shiva; the child Astika (son of Vasuki's sister Manasa) was conceived with the feeling of Shiva and intervened, and Pulla pacified Parashara. Thereafter all nagas promised: whoever takes the name of Astika, we will not bite. The upadesha: recite "Astika munirajaya namo nama" twice.
- Snake double-tongued: mantras with double repetition remove Ashlesha dosha and all poison. The Mahamantra (Hare Krishna, Hare Krishna, Krishna Krishna, Hare Hare...) is designed to remove Ashlesha dosha. Any planet in Ashlesha → do the Mahamantra.
- Dagda rashi / Dagda tithi: do a Rudra Abhishek. If Venus is combust, do Devi Abhishek to remove the combustion.
- Originally there were 28 nakshatras; Abhijit had a powerful "double Surya" (Paramatma) and got combusted coming down → 27. The Sun's own nakstra is also burnt → 26; Mars' nakshatra is boiling; Ketu's nakshatra is destroyed by infrared → only smoke. So 27 minus three Agni planets = only 24 nakshatras remain usable in a horoscope. Shani/Rahu create storms/hurricanes but do not destroy nakshatras (nakshatras are watery, ruled by the Moon). Doing the Gayatri mantra keeps your 24 nakshatras and your sanity intact — Agni will not destroy more nakshatras.

**41.1 Guru mudra and the 10-finger karma pattern**
- The teacher gives a sacred hand sequence in which the thumb touches each finger in sequence while the mantra "Om Gurave Namah" is recited, ending with the head-touch and the shiva-linga mudra at the chest.
- This is interpreted as a symbolic path through the 10th-house karma pattern, the 11th-house guru, and the 12th-house moksha orientation.
- The practical idea is that the hand and body become a living yantra: the fingers represent the karmic body, the head represents guru/brahma-shakti, and the chest represents the anahata offering to Krishna or the chosen avatar.

**41.2 Narada and tapasya as the model for discipline**
- Narada Muni is treated as the highest example of tapasya and bhakti. The story of Kamadeva's arrows is used to show that external desire cannot disturb a disciplined and protected bhakta.
- The teacher contrasts Narada's disciplined tapasya with the tendency of students to be proud, ahankari, and overconfident when they get a few predictions right.
- The central lesson is that jyotish is a difficult spiritual practice, not just a technical exercise or commercial skill.

**41.3 Chandas, mantra geometry, and the path of devotion**
- The teacher emphasizes that mantra is not just recitation; it must be formed in the correct chandas and structure. The Mahamantra is linked to the 32-syllable rhythm and the four-kendra pattern of the body and the cosmos.
- He explains that the basic mantra architecture imitates the structure of the body, the four directions, and the four kriya centers of life.
- This is framed as the basis of how a disciple learns to carry the mantra in the body rather than only in the mouth.

**41.4 Morning discipline, guru relationship, and the mind**
- The teacher insists on waking before sunrise, doing mantra before touching the ground, and keeping the mind anchored to the ista-devata or diksha-guru.
- He emphasizes that sleep itself is a kind of practice of death, so the habit of invoking mantra before sleep and after waking is one of the strongest spiritual habits.
- The mind is treated as a precious instrument and must be offered to Krishna, Vedavyasa, or the chosen avatar rather than to ordinary social, emotional, or egoic impulses.

**41.5 Guru-parampara and the five-level transmission**
- The teacher explains the transmission stream: Sadashiva → paratpara guru → paramaguru → guru → jiva. The point is not a formal list of names but the living reality of shakti flowing through the lineage.
- He teaches that mantra should be recited with proper sound, correct pronunciation, and recognition of the lineage obligations.
- The guru is not merely a teacher of technique; he is a transmitter of living discipline, ritual memory, and shakti-bearing orientation.

**41.6 Practical conclusion of the lecture**
- The lecture frames mantra, guru worship, disciplined body practice, and tapasya as inseparable from jyotish itself.
- The deeper teaching is that a chart is not only a map of karma but also a field of spiritual discipline. Practice, humility, and proper transmission matter as much as calculation.
- The teacher leaves the student with a practical formula: do the mantra with discipline, remember the guru, protect the mind, and live the daily habits that convert knowledge into embodied wisdom.

**42.1 The mantra as dharana and system tuning**
- The mantra "Om Gurave Namah" is treated as a tuning or centering practice. It stabilizes the system, sharpens concentration, and brings the seeker into a state of dharana rather than distracted movement.
- The teacher links this to the practical spiritual condition of jyotish: the chart is not merely an intellectual display but a field of attentiveness, order, and discipline.
- The mantra is used as a personal diksha or initiation formula, which is why it becomes especially powerful in the speaker's own practice.

**42.2 Moon in lagna is not a blanket good sign**
- The teacher stresses that Moon in lagna is only favorable when the sign, tithi, and paksha are supportive.
- In Cancer, Taurus, or Aries, Moon in lagna can produce beauty, richness, comfort, and a stable mental center.
- In other signs it can produce extreme passion, base tendencies, mental disturbance, deafness, dullness, or decline depending on the planetary environment.

**42.3 The Moon as mind-body driver**
- The Moon is described as the mind, the manas, the source of emotional life, and the captain of the body.
- The second and 12th houses from the Moon are critical for work ethic and marriage/relationship stability respectively.
- The 7th from the Moon is treated as the most important emotional axis, because it reveals the natural desire and response of the mind to life, time, and relationship.

**42.4 Work, marriage, and the 2nd/12th houses from the Moon**
- The 2nd from the Moon is the work orientation, the attitude toward earning, and the practical mind’s relationship to labor.
- The 12th from the Moon is the private emotional life, the tendencies toward marriage, intimacy, and emotional bonding.
- The teacher insists that without reading these houses, marriage and work predictions will fail because the mind itself is misaligned.

**42.5 Moon, Ketu, and Grahana logic**
- Ketu with the Moon produces Grahana-type mental extremism and dharma conflict; Moon with Ketu is not treated as a neutral combination.
- The teacher connects this to fear, ideologically intense states, and situations where the mind seeks a radical or destructive path.
- He also interprets the Sun–Rahu conjunction as a similar eclipse pattern, with Jupiter acting as the stabilizer or repairer of that eclipse.

**42.6 Practical conclusion**
- The speaker's core teaching is that the Moon governs the mind, not just emotional weather; therefore, Moon-sign and Moon-house logic must be read with the body, the chakra system, and the dasha timing in view.
- The lecture closes by tying the Moon to the spiritual path: the mind must be disciplined, the guru must be recognized, and the chart must be used as a guide to the right direction rather than as a detached technical exercise.
